            if v257 then
                v257 = v255:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if v256 and v257 then
                    getgenv(v252).AIFinalTarget = nil
                else
                    getgenv(v253).AIFinalTarget = nil
                end
                v258 = getgenv
            elseif v256 and v257 then
                getgenv(v252).AIFinalTarget = nil
                v258 = getgenv
            else
                getgenv(v254).AIFinalTarget = nil
                v258 = getgenv
            end
        else
            v257 = v255
            if v257 then
                v257 = v255:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if v256 and v257 then
                    getgenv(v252).AIFinalTarget = nil
                else
                    getgenv(v253).AIFinalTarget = nil
                end
                v258 = getgenv
            elseif v256 and v257 then
                getgenv(v252).AIFinalTarget = nil
                v258 = getgenv
            else
                getgenv().AIFinalTarget = nil
                v258 = getgenv
            end
        end
        v258().CachedWaypoints = nil
        return v250, v255, v256, v257
    end
end

                        end
                    else
                        v257, v252 = playerGui(v255, v256)
                        if v257 == nil then
                            break
                        else
                            v256 = v257
                            continue
                        end
                    end
                    break
                end
            end
            playerGui = player2:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
            if playerGui then
                v255, v256, v257 = ipairs(playerGui:GetDescendants())
                v252, v253 = v255(v256, v257)
                if not (v252 == nil) then
                    v257 = v252
                    while true do
                        if v253:IsA("TextLabel") or v253:IsA("TextButton") then
                            lower3 = string.lower(v253.Name)
                            if string.find(lower3, lower2) then
                                pcall(lower3, "amount")
                            end
                            v252, v253 = v255(v256, v257)
                            if v252 == nil then
                                v257 = v252
                            else
                                v257 = v252
                                continue
                            end
                        else
                            v252, v253 = v255(v256, v257)
                            if v252 == nil then
                                break
                            else
                                v257 = v252
                                continue
                            end
                        end
                        return v250, v251, lower2, v254, playerGui, v255, v256, v257, v252, v253
                    end
                end
                return v250, v251, lower2, v254, playerGui, v255, v256, v252, v252, v253
            else
                return v250, v251, lower2, v254, playerGui
            end
        end
    end
    player2 = game.Players.LocalPlayer
    if player2 then
        return player2, runProtected
    end
end
